For each term, contribution as function of α with possible B. We can compute Δ_p vector by adding contributions from four sets but ensure no duplicates. Terms involving i or j:

Can compute grad matrix current h quickly? Let's derive. For lag p with k=p-off. q_p = ∑_{r:s, r-s=k} h_r(1-h_s).

Could compute grad by looping over k (L ~2n-1) and assigning to slices:
for k, p:
  # first i with second i-k: i from max(0,k) to n-1+min(0,k)
  first_indices = arange(max(0,k), n+min(0,k))
  second_indices = first_indices - k
  grad[p, first_indices] += (1 - h[second_indices])
  # second i with first i+k: i from -? r=i+k valid => i from max(0,-k) to n-1+min(0,k); first r = i+k
  second_i = arange(max(0,-k), n+min(0,k))
  first_r = second_i + k
  grad[p, second_i] += -h[first_r]
This loops 499 times, assignments length ~n. O(499*250)=125k operations; if using Python loop with NumPy slices, maybe 0.1s? Could be 0.5s. Recompute after each accepted move may be too often. def local_search(h, n, deadline, min_time=5):
    L=2*n-1; off=n-1
    q = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    Cq = q.max(); dx=2/n
    grad = build_grad(h,n)
    # precompute pair arrays
    pair_i, pair_j = np.triu_indices(n,1)
    # pattern row arrays per variable? For grad update: for x, rows1[x]=off+arange(n)-x; rows2[x]=off+x-arange(n). Can precompute list of arrays? 2 n x n memory ~125k int, okay. Use tuple of arrays? Top-level helper maybe. In run, precompute rows_first, rows_second as 2D int arrays shape (n,n): rows_first[x,xcol]=off+xcol-x; rows_second[x,xcol]=off+x-xcol. Then update grad[rows_first[x], cols] -= a; grad[rows_second[x], cols] -= a. cols=arange(n). 2D int array ~250*250=62500 int64 ~0.5MB each, fine. For n up to 400 ~0.8MB.
```

def local_pass(h,q,grad,act,Cq,rowsF,rowsS,cols,deadline,active_resample=32):
    n=len(h); L=len(q); off=n-1
    pair_i,pair_j = precomputed? To avoid recompute each pass, pass.
    order = rng.permutation(len(pair_i)) maybe top-level? Need rng.
    no_improve_count=0
    accepts=0
    for idx in order:
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       i=pair_i[idx]; j=pair_j[idx]
       u=h[i]; v=h[j]
       lo = -min(u, 1-v)
       hi = min(1-u, v)
       if hi-lo < 1e-11: continue
       # skip if i/j at bounds and no room? hi/lo zero
       Aact = grad[act,i] - grad[act,j]
       qact = q[act]
       # Bact
       p1=off+i-j; p2=off+j-i
       Bact = ...
       a, pred = choose_alpha_active(Aact,qact,Bact,lo,hi)
       if pred < Cq - 1e-12:
           A = grad[:,i] - grad[:,j]
           a2=a*a
           tmp = A*a
           tmp[off] -= 2*a2
           tmp[p1] += a2
           tmp[p2] += a2
           cand = q + tmp
           newC = cand.max()
           if newC < Cq - 1e-13:
               q += tmp
               h[i]+=a; h[j]-=a
               grad[rowsF[i], cols] -= a
               grad[rowsF[j], cols] += a
               grad[rowsS[i], cols] -= a
               grad[rowsS[j], cols] += a
               Cq = newC
               accepts+=1
               if accepts % active_resample ==0 or newC < act_min? recompute act = np.argpartition(q, L-m)[-m:]
               # track if new max not in act? Maybe if accepts every update recompute? Sorting 499 each accept maybe okay if accepts few. If many, could be 250k*? Actually 250k sorts of 500 =125M log? Too much. Do every 16 and also if Cq changed significantly? Maybe newC index: idx=int(np.argmax(cand)); if new_idx not in act: recompute. Checking membership length m (8) trivial. If not, recompute. But cand already allocated; argmax returned new idx? `new_idx = int(np.argmax(cand))` inside cand.max?            else:
               no_improve +=1?
       # if many full eval no improve, break? 
    return...

```
def active_pred(A, qv, B, a, m):
    p = qv[0] + A[0]*a + B[0]*a*a
    for r in range(1,m):
       val = qv[r] + A[r]*a + B[r]*a*a
       if val > p: p=val
    return p
```

SA design with incremental state:
```
def sa_search(h,q,grad,deadline,rowsF,rowsS,cols,seed, start_h maybe):
   n,L,off; dx=2/n
   Cq=q.max()
   # initial temp by C5 scale: T0=0.05, Tmin=1e-6
   t_start=time.time(); t_end=deadline
   # measure step cost: do 50 random small? Maybe skip.
   # number of iterations: use until time.
   while time < deadline:
      progress = (time - start)/(elapsed budget)
      T = T0 * (1-progress)**2 + Tmin
      # choose pair random
      i = rng.randint(n); j = rng.randint(n); if i==j continue
      u=h[i]; v=h[j]
      lo=-min(u,1-v); hi=min(1-u,v)
      if hi-lo < 1e-8: continue
      # choose alpha: random mixture
      r=rng.random()
      if r < 0.7: a = lo + (hi-lo)*rng.random()
      else: # gradient-like: use active? maybe 0.01*(hi-lo) random sign
          a = (rng.random()*2-1)*0.05*(hi-lo)
          if a<lo: a=lo; if a>hi:a=hi
      A = grad[:,i]-grad[:,j]
      tmp=A*a; a2=a*a; tmp[off]-=2*a2; tmp[p1]+=a2; tmp[p2]+=a2
      cand=q+tmp; newC=cand.max()
      dC = (newC - Cq)*dx
      accept = newC < Cq or rng.random() < math.exp(-dC/T) if dC>0 else True
      if accept:
         q += tmp; h[i]+=a; h[j]-=a; update grad; Cq=newC
   return h,q,grad,Cq
```

def apply_transfer(h,q,grad,rowsF,rowsS,cols,i,j,a,off):
   n=len(h)
   A=grad[:,i]-grad[:,j]
   a2=a*a; p1=off+i-j; p2=off+j-i
   # Need tmp? For SA maybe we need tmp. We can use a preallocated tmp? Top-level cannot allocate? It can.
   tmp = A*a
   tmp[off] -= 2*a2; tmp[p1]+=a2; tmp[p2]+=a2
   cand = q + tmp
   newC = float(cand.max())
   idx = int(np.argmax(cand))
   q += tmp
   h[i]+=a; h[j]-=a
   grad[rowsF[i], cols] -= a
   grad[rowsS[i], cols] -= a
   grad[rowsF[j], cols] += a
   grad[rowsS[j], cols] += a
   return newC, idx
```

Project function iterative water filling:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
 target=n/2
 h=np.clip(h.copy(),0,1)
 for _ in range(1000):
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
   if diff>0:
      room=1-h; tot=room.sum()
      if tot<1e-15: break
      # add proportionally only to room: h += diff*room/total
      h += diff*room/tot
   else:
      h += diff*h/h.sum()
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 # final fix tiny: if sum not exact, adjust one mid value
 s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
 # find index not at bound
 for i in range(n):
   if h[i]>1e-12 and h[i]<1-1e-12:
      delta=np.clip(diff, -h[i]+? )
      h[i]+=delta; break
 return h
```

Implementation:
```
def choose_active_alpha(A, qv, B, lo, hi):
    m = len(A)
    cands = [lo, hi, 0.0]
    # mean
    sa = 0.0
    for r in range(m): sa += float(A[r])
    if abs(sa) > 1e-15: cands.append(-sa/m)
    # individual minima for B>0
    for r in range(m):
       br=float(B[r])
       if br > 1e-12 and abs(2*br)>...:
          cands.append(-float(A[r])/(2*br))
       elif br < -1e-12:
          cands.append(lo); cands.append(hi) # already
    # intersections
    for r in range(m):
       ar=float(A[r]); br=float(B[r]); qr=float(qv[r])
       for s in range(r+1,m):
          as_=float(A[s]); bs=...; ds=...
          db=br-bs; da=ar-as_; dq=qr-qv[s]
          if abs(db) < 1e-14:
             if abs(da)>1e-14: cands.append(-dq/da)
          else:
             disc = da*da - 4*db*dq
             if disc >= -1e-12:
                if disc<0: disc=0
                sqrt=Math.sqrt? math.sqrt
                denom=2*db
                cands.append((-da+sqrt)/denom)
                cands.append((-da-sqrt)/denom)
    best_a=0.0; best_pred=1e100
    for a in cands:
       if not np.isfinite(a): continue
       if a < lo: a=lo
       elif a > hi: a=hi
       aa=a*a; p=qv[0]+A[0]*a+B[0]*aa
       for r in range(1,m):
          val=qv[r]+A[r]*a+B[r]*aa
          if val>p: p=val
       if p<best_pred:
          best_pred=p; best_a=a
    return float(best_a), best_pred
```
Need import math as math. Use `math.sqrt`.
Duplicates: cands list may grow ~2+2+28=32. Evaluating clones okay. For m=12, intersections 66, roots 132, total 136; per pair 31k =>4M predictions, m=12 =>50M ops; maybe a bit heavy per pass. Use mact=8 to reduce: intersections28, roots56, total60, 31k*60*8=15M, better. Use mact=8 or 10. Maybe 8 enough. Let's use 8. If many tied max, top8 may omit; but recompute and full check. Now `sa_sweep` top-level:
```
def sa_sweep(h,q,grad,rowsF,rowsS,cols,rng,T0,Tmin,end_limit,off):
   n=len(h); L=len(q); dx=2.0/n
   Cq=float(q.max())
   tmp=np.empty(L); cand=np.empty(L)
   t_start=time.time(); total=max(1,end_limit-t_start)
   steps=0
   while time.time() < end_limit:
      progress=(time.time()-t_start)/total
      T = T0*(1-progress)**2 + Tmin
      i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
      if i==j: continue
      u=float(h[i]); v=float(h[j])
      lo=-min(u, 1.0-v); hi=min(1.0-u, v)
      if hi - lo < 1e-10: continue
      r=rng.random()
      if r < 0.65:
          a = lo + (hi-lo)*rng.random()
      elif r < 0.85:
          a = (rng.random()*2-1)*0.1*(hi-lo)
          if a<lo: a=lo
          elif a>hi: a=hi
      else:
          a = (rng.random()*2-1)*0.02
          if a<lo: a=lo
          elif a>hi: a=hi
      p1=off+i-j; p2=off+j-i
      np.subtract(grad[:,i], grad[:,j], out=tmp); tmp *= a; a2=a*a; tmp[off]-=2*a2; tmp[p1]+=a2; tmp[p2]+=a2
      np.add(q,tmp,out=cand)
      newC = float(cand.max())
      dC = (newC - Cq)*dx
      acc=False
      if newC < Cq:
         acc=True
      elif dC <= 0:
         acc=True
      else:
         # avoid underflow
         if dC/T < 700: acc = rng.random() < math.exp(-dC/T)
      if acc:
         q += tmp; h[i]+=a; h[j]-=a
         grad[rowsF[i],cols]-=a; grad[rowsS[i],cols]-=a
         grad[rowsF[j],cols]+=a; grad[rowsS[j],cols]+=a
         Cq=newC
      steps +=1
   return Cq
```
Need if T very small and dC large, exp underflow; check. Use `if dC/T < 50`? 50 enough. Use 700. math.exp.

Random h at potential. Step size absolute 0.02 maybe too small. For n=250, h values; a 0.02 transfer changes sum? okay. Could use `0.05*hi-lo` plus absolute.

Since local may polish worse and end worse. In `refine`, we can maintain `best_h = h.copy(); best_C5 = C5`. Before SA, copy current state? h copy length n cheap. If final C5 > best, restore. But local after SA may improve below. To avoid losing, after SA if C5 > best_C5, restore `h.copy()` and q/grad from best? We didn't save q/grad. We can just at end of refine evaluate h; if worse than best_h saved, return best_h. But q/grad state being worse okay. For subsequent SA/local, starting from worse maybe not. We can restore h to best and recompute q/grad if better not improved. Simpler: In refine, after SA if current C5 not <= best_C5 - 1e-12, set h=best_h.copy(), recompute q,grad,Cq. But recompute. def refine(h_start,n,deadline,rng,sa_chunk=30.0,sa_T0=0.003,local_limit=30.0):
   h=project...
   q=...; Cq=...; C5=Cq*dx
   grad=build_grad...
   info precompute
   best_h=h.copy(); best_C5=C5
   stagnant=0
   while time.time() < deadline-5:
      # local segment until local_limit or no improvement
      local_deadline = min(deadline-5, time.time()+local_limit)
      act = recompute_act(q,L,mact)
      Cq, act, improved = local_sweep(..., Cq, rng, local_deadline, ...)
      C5=Cq*dx
      if C5 < best_C5:
         best_C5=C5; best_h=h.copy(); stagnant=0
      else:
         stagnant +=1
      if time.time() >= deadline-5: break
      if not improved or stagnant > 3:
         # SA segment

         T0 = max(0.0004, 0.003 * (0.5**stagnant)) maybe

         else:
             best_h=h.copy(); best_C5=C5
         stagnant=0
   # final local until deadline-2
   local_deadline=deadline-2

One subtlety: `grad[rowsF[i], cols] -= a` with advanced indexing and `cols` array of int: It does not accumulate if there are duplicate index pairs within the two arrays. There are none. If duplicates, it wouldn't accumulate properly, but no.

`rowsF` and `rowsS` as 2D int arrays shape (n,n):
```
cols = np.arange(n)
idx = cols[None, :]
rowsF = off + idx - cols[:, None]  # shape (n,n): for variable x row entry col c = off + c - x
rowsS = off + cols[:, None] - idx
```
Here first dimension x (variable), second col c. Need ensure type int. `off + idx - cols[:,None]` yields int. Good.

In `build_grad`, use `h` values. If called after h maybe not float? cast.
```
def build_grad(h,n):
 L=2*n-1; off=n-1
 grad=np.zeros((L,n),float)
 cols=np.arange(n)
 for k in range(-(n-1), n):
   p=off+k
   start=max(0,k); end=min(n-1,n-1+k)
   if start<=end:
      c=cols[start:end+1]
      grad[p,c] += 1.0 - h[c-k]
   start=max(0,-k); end=min(n-1,n-1-k)
   if start<=end:
      c=cols[start:end+1]
      grad[p,c] -= h[c+k]
 return grad
```
Check ranges for k negative: first end = n-1+k (e.g., n=5,k=-2 end=2), start0; first c=0..2, c-k=2..4 valid. second start=2,end=n-1-(-2)=6 min4=>4; c=2..4, c+k=0..2 valid. Good.
This loops 2n-1 up to 499; each uses slice. Good.

`project_feasible`: If h sum far, one iteration enough? Use:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
 h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 target=n/2.0
 for _ in range(100):
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
   if diff>0:
      room=1.0-h; tot=room.sum()
      if tot <= 1e-15: break
      h += diff * room / tot
   else:
      tot=h.sum()
      if tot <= 1e-15: break
      h += diff * h / tot
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 # final micro-adjust
 diff=target-h.sum()
 if abs(diff)>1e-14:
    # find index with slack
    for i in range(n):
       if 1e-12 < h[i] < 1-1e-12:
          h[i] += diff
          h[i] = min(1.0, max(0.0, h[i]))
          break
    else:
       # if no slack, distribute? This only if at bounds but sum wrong; average
       h[:] += diff / n
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 return h
```
The proportional update with diff/ tot may still leave diff due to floating; one loop enough. The final micro adjust may change sum if h[i] bound clips; but diff small.

Need ensure h used for q not NaN. If initial_h_values perhaps None? Use try.

```
def recompute_act(q, L, mact):
    if L <= mact: return np.arange(L)
    return np.argpartition(q, L-mact)[L-mact:]  # top mact unsorted
```
But `np.argpartition(q, kth)[-mact:]` yields last mact elements (largest) but unsorted. Use `np.argpartition(q, L-mact)[-mact:]`. If L-mact==0 (mact>=L) handle. If L-mact < L okay. For kth maybe should be L-mact (exclusive). Yes.

`local_sweep` signature maybe long. We'll implement top-level:
```
def local_sweep(h, q, grad, rowsF, rowsS, cols, pairs, act, Cq, rng, limit, off, mact):
    pair_i, pair_j = pairs
    n = len(h)
    L = len(q)
    Aact = np.empty(mact, float)
    Bact = np.empty(mact, float)
    tmp = np.empty(L, float)
    cand = np.empty(L, float)
    improved = False
    order = rng.permutation(len(pair_i))
    accepted = 0
    for idx in order:
       if time.time() > limit: break
       i = int(pair_i[idx]); j = int(pair_j[idx])
       u = float(h[i]); v = float(h[j])
       lo = -min(u, 1.0-v)
       hi = min(1.0-u, v)
       if hi - lo < 1e-11: continue
       # Aact
       Aact[:] = grad[act, i]
       Aact -= grad[act, j]
       # qact maybe view? We pass q[act] to choose; it copies? choose accesses. Could use `qv = q[act]`.
       qv = q[act]
       p1 = off + i - j; p2 = off + j - i
       Bact[:] = 0.0
       for r in range(mact):
           p = int(act[r])
           if p == off: Bact[r] = -2.0
           if p == p1: Bact[r] += 1.0
           if p == p2: Bact[r] += 1.0
       a, pred = choose_active_alpha(Aact, qv, Bact, lo, hi)
       if pred >= Cq - 1e-12: continue
       np.subtract(grad[:, i], grad[:, j], out=tmp)
       a2 = a*a
       tmp *= a
       tmp[off] -= 2*a2
       tmp[p1] += a2
       tmp[p2] += a2
       np.add(q, tmp, out=cand)
       new_idx = int(cand.argmax())
       newC = float(cand[new_idx])
       if newC < Cq - 1e-13:
           q += tmp
           h[i] += a; h[j] -= a
           grad[rowsF[i], cols] -= a
           grad[rowsS[i], cols] -= a
           grad[rowsF[j], cols] += a
           grad[rowsS[j], cols] += a
           Cq = newC
           improved = True
           accepted += 1
           if (accepted & 15) == 0 or not np.any(act == new_idx):
               if L <= mact:
                  act = np.arange(L)
               else:
                  act = np.argpartition(q, L-mact)[-mact:]
           if accepted % 5000 == 0:
               q[:] = np.correlate(h, 1-h, mode='full')
               Cq = float(q.max())
               grad[:] = build_grad(h, n)
               act = np.argpartition(q, L-mact)[-mact:] if L>mact else np.arange(L)
    return act, Cq, improved
```
But issue: `Aact[:] = grad[act, i]` if act length mact but grad advanced indexing returns copy length mact. If L<mact? mact = min(8,L). Good. If `act` recomputed with length L (if L<=mact), okay.
Potential issue: `np.subtract(grad[:,i], grad[:,j], out=tmp)` uses grad columns; if grad is modified later, tmp delta from old grad. Good.
If `a` is very small (e.g., 1e-16) and pred improves by < tol, maybe not; skip if `abs(a) < 1e-15`? Could avoid no-op. Add if `abs(a) < 1e-14: continue` after choose? But if hi-lo tiny skip. If a near 0, no improvement. Add.
`pred` could be NaN if choose gets NaN? Not if q finite.
`not np.any(act == new_idx)` scans act. If act has newline? okay.
When recompute active, length mact. Bact/Aact sizes fixed mact. If L <= mact, act length L, but mact variable defined as min(8,L). If L<mact? We set mact=min(8,L), so act length=mact always.

def refine(h_start, n, deadline, rng, sa_chunk=30.0, local_limit=25.0, T0_base=0.002):
   ...
   stagnant=0
   while time.time() < deadline - 8:
       local_limit_use = local_limit
       if deadline - time.time() < local_limit_use + 5: local_limit_use = max(5, deadline-time()-5)
       act=C5? recompute
       act,Cq,improved,completed = local_sweep(..., local_deadline, ...)
       C5=Cq*dx
       if C5 < best_C5:
           best_C5=C5; best_h=h.copy(); stagnant=0
       elif completed and not improved:
           stagnant += 1
       # if not completed and improved maybe continue; if not completed no SA
       if time.time() >= deadline - 8: break
       if completed and (not improved or stagnant >= 3):
           sa_end = min(deadline - 8, time.time() + sa_chunk)
           T0 = min(0.03, T0_base * (1.5 ** stagnant))
           T0 = max(T0, 0.0005)
           # save best in h already
           Cq = sa_sweep(..., T0, 1e-5, sa_end, off)
           C5=Cq*dx
           if C5 > best_C5 + 1e-12:
               h = best_h.copy()
               q = np.correlate(h, 1-h, 'full')
               Cq=float(q.max()); grad=build_grad(h,n); C5=best_C5
           else:
               best_C5=C5; best_h=h.copy()
           stagnant=0
   # final local
   if time.time() < deadline - 2:
      act=...
      local_sweep(... deadline-2)
   # final projection and eval
   h = project_feasible(h,n)
   c5 = c5_value(h)
   if best_h is not None:
       bh = project_feasible(best_h,n)
       bc = c5_value(bh)
       if bc < c5: h,best_c = bh,bc
       else: c5=bc? Actually if c5 from maybe worse; choose min.
   return h, c5
```

```
def recompute_act(q, L, mact, tol_q=1e-4):
   thresh = q.max() - tol_q
   idx = np.where(q >= thresh)[0]
   if len(idx) <= mact:
       return idx
   # if too many, take top mact by q
   return np.argpartition(q, L-mact)[-mact:]
```

Starts generation:
```
def alternate_starts(h_best, n_best, rng, max_per? ):
   starts=[]
   # threshold
   th = np.zeros(n_best); order=np.argsort(h_best)[::-1]; th[order[:n_best//2]]=1.0
   starts.append((th,n_best))
   # periodic if n even
   if n_best % 2 == 0:
      m=n_best//2
      per=np.concatenate((h_best[:m], h_best[:m]))
      anti=np.concatenate((h_best[:m], 1-h_best[:m]))
      starts.append((per,n_best)); starts.append((anti,n_best))
   # smooth random
   for _ in range(2):
      x=np.linspace(0,2,n_best,endpoint=False)
      h=0.5 + 0.2*np.sin(2*np.pi*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)) + 0.1*np.sin(4*np.pi*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
      starts.append((project_feasible(np.clip(h,0,1),n_best), n_best))
   # random binary maybe
   r = np.zeros(n_best); r[rng.permutation(n_best)[:n_best//2]]=1; starts.append((r,n_best))
```
Need avoid too many. Use up to 3.

Time allocation in run: We can simply call refine in a loop with deadlines, and let it use remaining. But refine currently uses its own local/SA until deadline given. If we give it all for first, no alternate. We can pass subdeadlines.

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100):
 t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-5
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 # parent
 try: h0=np.array(initial_h_values,float).copy(); n0=len(h0)
 except: n0=250; h0=default_h(n0,rng)
 h0=project_feasible(h0,n0)
 c0=c5_value(h0)
 best_h=h0; best_c=c0; best_n=n0
 # first refine: allocate 55%? e.g. 600s
 d1=min(deadline, t0 + max(60, budget_s*0.45))
 h,c=refine(h0,n0,d1,rng, T0_base=0.002, local_limit=25, sa_chunk=25)
 if c<best_c: update
 # alternate if time
 starts = make_alternates(best_h,best_n,rng)
 for hst,nst in starts:
    rem=deadline-time.time()
    if rem < 80: break
    alloc=min(150, rem*0.25) # not all
    d=t0? time.time()+alloc
    h,c=refine(hst,nst,d,rng, T0_base=0.01, local_limit=15, sa_chunk=15)
    if c<best_c: update
 # final
 rem=deadline-time.time()
 if rem > 20:
    d=deadline-2
    h,c=refine(best_h,best_n,d,rng, T0_base=0.0008, local_limit=20, sa_chunk=10)
    if c<best_c: update
 # ensure best feasible and c
 best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
 best_c=c5_value(best_h)
 return best_h,best_c,best_n
```

```
def alternate_starts(h_best,n_best,rng):
   res=[]
   # threshold
   th=np.zeros(n_best)
   if n_best>1:
      order=np.argsort(h_best)[::-1]
      th[order[:n_best//2]]=1.0
   res.append((th,n_best))
   # periodic/anti if even
   if n_best % 2 == 0:
      m=n_best//2
      first=h_best[:m].copy()
      per=np.concatenate((first, first))
      anti=np.concatenate((first, 1.0-first))
      res.append((project_feasible(per,n_best), n_best))
      res.append((project_feasible(anti,n_best), n_best))
   # random binary
   b=np.zeros(n_best)
   if n_best>1:
      b[rng.permutation(n_best)[:n_best//2]]=1.0
   res.append((b,n_best))
   # smooth random
   x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_best,endpoint=False)
   for _ in range(2):
      ph1=rng.uniform(0.0,2*math.pi)
      ph2=rng.uniform(0.0,2*math.pi)
      h=0.5 + 0.25*np.sin(2*math.pi*x+ph1) + 0.12*np.sin(4*math.pi*x+ph2)
      res.append((project_feasible(np.clip(h,0.1,1.0),n_best), n_best))
   # coarse
   if n_best > 150:
      m=n_best//2
      xf=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_best,endpoint=False)
      xc=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,m,endpoint=False)
      hc=np.interp(xc,xf,h_best)
      res.append((project_feasible(hc,m), m))
   return res
```

```
import numpy as np
import math
import time

def project_feasible(h,n): ...
def c5_value(h): ...
def build_grad(h,n): ...
def recompute_act(q,L,mact): ...
def choose_active_alpha(A,qv,B,lo,hi): ...
def local_sweep(...): ...
def sa_sweep(...): ...
def default_h(n,rng): ...
def alternate_starts(h,n,rng): ...
def refine(...): ...

Need ensure no closure/lambda.

Let's detail `refine` code:
```
def refine(h_start,n,deadline,rng,T0_base=0.002,local_limit=25.0,sa_chunk=25.0,mact=8):
    h = project_feasible(h_start, n)
    dx=2.0/n
    q = np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    L=2*n-1; off=n-1
    Cq=float(q.max())
    grad=build_grad(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_C5=Cq*dx
    cols=np.arange(n)
    rowsF = off + cols[None,:] - cols[:,None]
    rowsS = off + cols[:,None] - cols[None,:]
    pair_i,pair_j=np.triu_indices(n,1)
    if L <= mact:
       mact = L
    act=recompute_act(q,L,mact)
    stagnant=0
    while time.time() < deadline - 8:
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        ll = min(local_limit, rem - 5.0)
        if ll < 5.0: ll = max(1.0, rem - 5.0)
        if ll <= 0.0: break
        local_deadline = time.time() + ll
        act, Cq, improved, completed = local_sweep(h,q,grad,rowsF,rowsS,cols,pair_i,pair_j,act,Cq,rng,local_deadline,off,mact)
        C5=Cq*dx
        if C5 < best_C5 - 1e-15:
            best_C5=C5; best_h=h.copy(); stagnant=0
        elif completed and not improved:
            stagnant += 1
        if time.time() >= deadline - 8: break
        if completed and (not improved or stagnant >= 3):
            rem2=deadline-time.time()
            if rem2 > 25.0:
                sa_end = min(deadline - 8, time.time() + sa_chunk)
                T0 = T0_base * (1.5 ** stagnant)
                if T0 < 0.0005: T0=0.0005
                if T0 > 0.03: T0=0.03
                Cq = sa_sweep(h,q,grad,rowsF,rowsS,cols,rng,T0,1e-5,sa_end,off)
                C5=Cq*dx
                if C5 > best_C5 + 1e-12:
                    h = best_h.copy()
                    q = np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
                    Cq=float(q.max()); grad=build_grad(h,n); C5=best_C5
                else:
                    best_C5=C5; best_h=h.copy(); C5=Cq*dx
                stagnant=0
    if time.time() < deadline - 2:
        act=recompute_act(q,L,mact)
        local_sweep(h,q,grad,rowsF,rowsS,cols,pair_i,pair_j,act,Cq,rng,deadline-2.0,off,mact)
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    c5=c5_value(h)
    bh = project_feasible(best_h,n)
    bc = c5_value(bh)
    if bc < c5:
        h,b c = bh,bc
    return h, bc
```
Potential bug: In `rowsF = off + cols[None,:] - cols[:,None]`, `cols[None,:]` shape (1,n), `cols[:,None]` shape (n,1), off scalar; result shape (n,n). Here first dimension variable x, second col c. For variable x row, entry c: off+c-x. Good.
`rowsS = off + cols[:,None] - cols[None,:]`: variable x, col c: off+x-c. Good.

Before final, let's review line by line for syntax.

```
import numpy as np
import math
import time

def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = n / 2.0
    for _ in range(100):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h += diff * room / tot
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h += diff * h / tot
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        placed = False
        for i in range(n):
            if 1e-12 < h[i] < 1.0 - 1e-12:
                h[i] += diff
                h[i] = min(1.0, max(0.0, h[i]))
                placed = True
                break
        if not placed:
            h[:] += diff / max(1, n)
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h
```
The final micro adjust if h[i]+diff out clips, sum may not exact. But diff tiny. Good.

```
def c5_value(h):
    n=len(h)
    if n==0: return 0.0
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')) * (2.0/n))
```

`recompute_act`:
```
def recompute_act(q, L, mact):
    if mact >= L:
        return np.argsort(q)[::-1]
    idx = np.argpartition(q, L - mact)[-mact:]
    idx = idx[np.argsort(q[idx])[::-1]]
    return idx
```
If L=0? not.

`choose_active_alpha`: Need if cands empty? It starts with boundaries/0. If lo/hi maybe both 0; cands duplicates. best_pred initialize. If all candidates nonfinite? boundaries finite. Good.
```
def choose_active_alpha(A, qv, B, lo, hi):
    m=len(A)
    cands=[lo,hi,0.0]
    s=0.0
    for r in range(m): s += float(A[r])
    if abs(s) > 1e-15: cands.append(-s/m)
    for r in range(m):
       br=float(B[r])
       if br > 1e-12:
          cands.append(-float(A[r])/(2.0*br))
    for r in range(m):
       ar=float(A[r]); br=float(B[r]); qr=float(qv[r])
       for s_ in range(r+1,m):
          as_=float(A[s_]); bs=float(B[s_]); ds=float(qv[s_])
          db=br-bs; da=ar-as_; dq=qr-ds
          if abs(db) < 1e-14:
             if abs(da) > 1e-14:
                cands.append(-dq/da)
          else:
             disc=da*da - 4.0*db*dq
             if disc >= -1e-12:
                if disc < 0.0: disc=0.0
                sqrt=math.sqrt(disc)
                den=2.0*db
                cands.append((-da + sqrt)/den)
                cands.append((-da - sqrt)/den)
    best_a=0.0; best_pred=1e100
    for raw in cands:
       try:
          a=float(raw)
       except Exception:
          continue
       if not math.isfinite(a): continue
       if a < lo: a=lo
       elif a > hi: a=hi
       aa=a*a
       p=float(qv[0]) + float(A[0])*a + float(B[0])*aa
       for r in range(1,m):
          val=float(qv[r]) + float(A[r])*a + float(B[r])*aa
          if val > p: p=val
       if p < best_pred:
          best_pred=p; best_a=a
    return best_a, best_pred
```
The variable `s` reused in outer? We used `s` for sum, then inner `s_` good. But after sum, `s` remains; no issue. In intersection loop, `ds` maybe conflict with dx? fine.
Could convert A[qv] to floats many times; okay.

completed=False

```
import numpy as np
import math
import time

def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = n / 2.0
    for _ in range(100):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h += diff * room / tot
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h += diff * h / tot
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        for i in range(n):
            if 1e-12 < h[i] < 1.0 - 1e-12:
                h[i] = min(1.0, max(0.0, h[i] + diff))
                break
        else:
            h = np.clip(h + diff / max(1, n), 0.0, 1.0)
    return h
...
```
The final else if no slack, `h + diff / n` may push bounds; project. Good.

`build_grad` compact:
```
def build_grad(h, n):
    L = 2*n - 1
    off = n - 1
    grad = np.zeros((L, n), float)
    cols = np.arange(n)
    for k in range(-(n-1), n):
        p = off + k
        a = max(0, k); b = min(n-1, n-1+k)
        if a <= b:
            c = cols[a:b+1]
            grad[p, c] += 1.0 - h[c - k]
        a = max(0, -k); b = min(n-1, n-1-k)
        if a <= b:
            c = cols[a:b+1]
            grad[p, c] -= h[c + k]
    return grad
```
Good.

`choose_active_alpha` compact maybe:
```
def choose_active_alpha(A, qv, B, lo, hi):
    m = len(A)
    cands = [lo, hi, 0.0]
    for r in range(m):
        if B[r] > 1e-12:
            cands.append(-A[r] / (2.0 * B[r]))
    for r in range(m):
        ar, br, qr = float(A[r]), float(B[r]), float(qv[r])
        for s in range(r+1, m):
            as_, bs, qs = float(A[s]), float(B[s]), float(qv[s])
            db = br - bs
            da = ar - as_
            dq = qr - qs
            if abs(db) < 1e-14:
                if abs(da) > 1e-14:
                    cands.append(-dq / da)
            else:
                disc = da*da - 4.0*db*dq
                if disc >= -1e-12:
                    sqrt = math.sqrt(max(0.0, disc))
                    den = 2.0 * db
                    cands.append((-da + sqrt) / den)
                    cands.append((-da - sqrt) / den)
    best_a, best_pred = 0.0, 1e100
    for raw in cands:
        try: a = float(raw)
        except Exception: continue
        if not math.isfinite(a): continue
        if a < lo: a = lo
        elif a > hi: a = hi
        aa = a*a
        p = float(qv[0]) + float(A[0])*a + float(B[0])*aa
        for r in range(1, m):
            v = float(qv[r]) + float(A[r])*a + float(B[r])*aa
            if v > p: p = v
        if p < best_pred:
            best_pred, best_a = p, a
    return best_a, best_pred
```
Removed mean; but if all slopes same, boundaries okay. Individual min only B>0. Fine.
Could candidate list have `B[r]` numpy scalar in `if B[r] >`, okay. `-A[r]/...` maybe np scalar appended. Good.

`local_sweep` compact. Need line count. We'll write with minimal blank.

```
def local_sweep(h, q, grad, rowsF, rowsS, cols, pair_i, pair_j, act, Cq, rng, limit, off, mact):
    n = len(h); L = len(q)
    Aact = np.empty(mact, float); Bact = np.empty(mact, float)
    Afull = np.empty(L, float); tmp = np.empty(L, float); cand = np.empty(L, float)
    improved = False; completed = False; accepted = 0
    order = rng.permutation(len(pair_i))
    factors = (1.0, 0.5, 0.2, 0.05)
    for idx in order:
        if time.time() > limit:
            break
        i = int(pair_i[idx]); j = int(pair_j[idx])
        u = float(h[i]); v = float(h[j])
        lo = -min(u, 1.0 - v); hi = min(1.0 - u, v)
        if hi - lo < 1e-11:
            continue
        p1 = off + i - j; p2 = off + j - i
        tolq = max(1e-3, 1e-4 * Cq)
        am = 0
        qmax = float(q[act[0]])
        for r in range(mact):
            if float(q[act[r]]) >= qmax - tolq:
                am += 1
            else:
                break
        if am < 1: am = 1
        active = act[:am]
        Aact[:am] = grad[active, i]
        Aact[:am] -= grad[active, j]
        qv = q[active]
        Bact[:am] = 0.0
        for r in range(am):
            p = int(active[r])
            if p == off: Bact[r] = -2.0
            if p == p1: Bact[r] += 1.0
            if p == p2: Bact[r] += 1.0
        a, pred = choose_active_alpha(Aact[:am], qv, Bact[:am], lo, hi)
        if pred >= Cq - 1e-12 or abs(a) < 1e-15:
            continue
        np.subtract(grad[:, i], grad[:, j], out=Afull)
        for f in factors:
            aa = a * f
            if abs(aa) < 1e-15:
                continue
            a2 = aa * aa
            tmp[:] = Afull
            tmp *= aa
            tmp[off] -= 2.0 * a2
            tmp[p1] += a2
            tmp[p2] += a2
            np.add(q, tmp, out=cand)
            new_idx = int(cand.argmax())
            newC = float(cand[new_idx])
            if newC < Cq - 1e-13:
                q += tmp
                h[i] += aa; h[j] -= aa
                grad[rowsF[i], cols] -= aa
                grad[rowsS[i], cols] -= aa
                grad[rowsF[j], cols] += aa
                grad[rowsS[j], cols] += aa
                Cq = newC; improved = True; accepted += 1
                if (accepted & 15) == 0 or int(act[0]) != new_idx:
                    act = recompute_act(q, L, mact)
                if accepted % 5000 == 0:
                    q[:] = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
                    Cq = float(q.max())
                    grad[:] = build_grad(h, n)
                    act = recompute_act(q, L, mact)
                break
    else:
        completed = True
    return act, Cq, improved, completed
```
Issue: `int(act[0]) != new_idx` if act size maybe 0? mact at least 1. Good.
If recompute act after accept, `new_idx` refers to old? fine. If accepted%5000, recompute again. Good.
If am < mact, Aact beyond stale. Fine.
One subtle bug: In full eval factors, if f=1 reject, then f=0.5, but `Afull` is gradient at old h. Since no state change, correct. If f=1 accepts, state changed and break factors. Good.
If f=1 rejects but f=0.5 accepts, active pred for f=1 was <Cq, but f=0.5 maybe not reduce active enough? Full checks. Good.

`sa_sweep` compact:
```
def sa_sweep(h, q, grad, rowsF, rowsS, cols, rng, T0, Tmin, end_limit, off):
    n = len(h); L = len(q); dx = 2.0/n
    Cq = float(q.max())
    tmp = np.empty(L, float); cand = np.empty(L, float)
    t0 = time.time(); total = max(1.0, end_limit - t0)
    rec = 0
    while time.time() < end_limit:
        prog = (time.time() - t0) / total
        if prog > 1.0: prog = 1.0
        T = T0 * (1.0 - prog) * (1.0 - prog) + Tmin
        i = rng.randint(n); j = rng.randint(n)
        if i == j: continue
        u = float(h[i]); v = float(h[j])
        lo = -min(u, 1.0 - v); hi = min(1.0 - u, v)
        if hi - lo < 1e-10: continue
        gap = hi - lo
        r = rng.random()
        if r < 0.7:
            a = lo + gap * rng.random()
        elif r < 0.9:
            a = (rng.random() * 2.0 - 1.0) * 0.1 * gap
            if a < lo: a = lo
            elif a > hi: a = hi
        else:
            a = (rng.random() * 2.0 - 1.0) * 0.02
            if a < lo: a = lo
            elif a > hi: a = hi
        p1 = off + i - j; p2 = off + j - i
        np.subtract(grad[:, i], grad[:, j], out=tmp)
        a2 = a*a
        tmp *= a
        tmp[off] -= 2.0*a2
        tmp[p1] += a2
        tmp[p2] += a2
        np.add(q, tmp, out=cand)
        new_idx = int(cand.argmax())
        newC = float(cand[new_idx])
        accept = False
        if newC < Cq:
            accept = True
        else:
            dC = (newC - Cq) * dx
            if dC <= 0.0:
                accept = True
            else:
                ratio = dC / T
                if ratio < 700.0 and rng.random() < math.exp(-ratio):
                    accept = True
        if accept:
            q += tmp
            h[i] += a; h[j] -= a
            grad[rowsF[i], cols] -= a
            grad[rowsS[i], cols] -= a
            grad[rowsF[j], cols] += a
            grad[rowsS[j], cols] += a
            Cq = newC
            rec += 1
            if rec & 4095 == 0:
                q[:] = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
                Cq = float(q.max())
                grad[:] = build_grad(h, n)
    return Cq
```
Good.

`default_h` compact.

`alternate_starts` compact:
```
def alternate_starts(h, n, rng):
    res = []
    th = np.zeros(n)
    if n:
        th[np.argsort(h)[::-1][:n//2]] = 1.0
    res.append((th, n))
    if n % 2 == 0:
        m = n//2
        first = h[:m].copy()
        res.append((project_feasible(np.concatenate((first, first)), n), n))
        res.append((project_feasible(np.concatenate((first, 1.0 - first)), n), n))
    rb = np.zeros(n)
    if n:
        rb[rng.permutation(n)[:n//2]] = 1.0
    res.append((rb, n))
    if n > 140:
        m = n//2
        xf = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
        xc = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, m, endpoint=False)
        res.append((project_feasible(np.interp(xc, xf, h), m), m))
    if n > 0:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
        h2 = 0.5 + 0.25*np.sin(2.0*math.pi*x + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*math.pi)) + 0.12*np.sin(4.0*math.pi*x + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*math.pi))
        res.append((project_feasible(np.clip(h2,0,1), n), n))
    return res
```
Could be 6 starts. In run limit 3.

`refine` compact: Need include `mact = min(mact, max(1,L))`.
```
def refine(h_start, n, deadline, rng, T0_base=0.002, local_limit=25.0, sa_chunk=25.0, mact=8):
    h = project_feasible(h_start, n)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    q = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    L = len(q); off = n - 1; Cq = float(q.max())
    grad = build_grad(h, n)
    mact = min(mact, L)
    best_h = h.copy(); best_C5 = Cq * dx
    cols = np.arange(n)
    rowsF = off + cols[None, :] - cols[:, None]
    rowsS = off + cols[:, None] - cols[None, :]
    pair_i, pair_j = np.triu_indices(n, 1)
    act = recompute_act(q, L, mact)
    stagnant = 0
    while time.time() < deadline - 8.0:
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        ll = min(local_limit, rem - 5.0)
        if ll < 1.0: break
        act, Cq, improved, completed = local_sweep(h, q, grad, rowsF, rowsS, cols, pair_i, pair_j, act, Cq, rng, time.time() + ll, off, mact)
        C5 = Cq * dx
        if C5 < best_C5 - 1e-15:
            best_C5 = C5; best_h = h.copy(); stagnant = 0
        elif completed and not improved:
            stagnant += 1
        if time.time() >= deadline - 8.0: break
        if completed and (not improved or stagnant >= 3):
            if deadline - time.time() > 20.0:
                T0 = min(0.03, max(0.0005, T0_base * (1.5 ** stagnant)))
                Cq = sa_sweep(h, q, grad, rowsF, rowsS, cols, rng, T0, 1e-5, min(deadline - 8.0, time.time() + sa_chunk), off)
                C5 = Cq * dx
                if C5 > best_C5 + 1e-12:
                    h = best_h.copy()
                    q = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
                    Cq = float(q.max()); grad = build_grad(h, n); C5 = best_C5
                else:
                    best_C5 = C5; best_h = h.copy()
                stagnant = 0
    if time.time() < deadline - 2.0:
        act = recompute_act(q, L, mact)
        local_sweep(h, q, grad, rowsF, rowsS, cols, pair_i, pair_j, act, Cq, rng, deadline - 2.0, off, mact)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c5 = c5_value(h)
    bh = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    bc = c5_value(bh)
    if bc < c5:
        return bh, bc
    return h, c5
```
Potential bug: If `n=1`, pair_i empty, rows arrays shape (1,1), local_sweep order empty completed True, local no improved; SA random i==j always continue; final. Fine.

`run`:
```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h0 = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = int(len(h0))
    except Exception:
        n0 = 250
        h0 = 0.5 + 0.25*np.sin(2.0*math.pi*np.arange(n0)/n0)
    if n0 < 2:
        n0 = 250
        h0 = 0.5 + 0.25*np.sin(2.0*math.pi*np.arange(n0)/n0)
    if n0 > 600:
        m = 300
        xf = np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n0,endpoint=False); xc=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,m,endpoint=False)
        h0 = np.interp(xc, xf, h0); n0=m
    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n0)
    best_h = h0; best_n = n0; best_c = c5_value(h0)
    total = max(10.0, deadline - t0)
    first_end = min(deadline, t0 + max(30.0, total * 0.40))
    h, c = refine(h0, n0, first_end, rng, T0_base=0.002, local_limit=25.0, sa_chunk=25.0)
    if c < best_c:
        best_h, best_n, best_c = h, n, c
    starts = alternate_starts(best_h, best_n, rng)
    alt = 0
    for hst, nst in starts:
        if alt >= 3: break
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 90.0: break
        alloc = min(140.0, max(35.0, rem * 0.22))
        end = min(deadline, time.time() + alloc)
        h, c = refine(hst, nst, end, rng, T0_base=0.008, local_limit=12.0, sa_chunk=12.0)
        if c < best_c:
            best_h, best_n, best_c = h, nst, c
        alt += 1
    if deadline - time.time() > 25.0:
        h, c = refine(best_h, best_n, deadline - 2.0, rng, T0_base=0.0008, local_limit=20.0, sa_chunk=10.0)
        if c < best_c:
            best_h, best_n, best_c = h, best_n, c
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = c5_value(best_h)
    return best_h, best_c, best_n
```

```python
import numpy as np
import math
import time

def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = n / 2.0
    for _ in range(100):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h += diff * room / tot
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h += diff * h / tot
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        for i in range(n):
            if 1e-12 < h[i] < 1.0 - 1e-12:
                h[i] = min(1.0, max(0.0, h[i] + diff))
                break
        else:
            h = np.clip(h + diff / max(1, n), 0.0, 1.0)
    return h

def c5_value(h):
    n = len(h)
    if n <= 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * (2.0 / n))

def build_grad(h, n):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    off = n - 1
    grad = np.zeros((L, n), float)
    cols = np.arange(n)
    for k in range(-(n - 1), n):
        p = off + k
        a = max(0, k); b = min(n - 1, n - 1 + k)
        if a <= b:
            c = cols[a:b + 1]
            grad[p, c] += 1.0 - h[c - k]
        a = max(0, -k); b = min(n - 1, n - 1 - k)
        if a <= b:
            c = cols[a:b + 1]
            grad[p, c] -= h[c + k]
    return grad

def recompute_act(q, L, mact):
    if mact >= L:
        return np.argsort(q)[::-1]
    idx = np.argpartition(q, L - mact)[-mact:]
    return idx[np.argsort(q[idx])[::-1]]

def choose_active_alpha(A, qv, B, lo, hi):
    m = len(A)
    cands = [lo, hi, 0.0]
    for r in range(m):
        if B[r] > 1e-12:
            cands.append(-float(A[r]) / (2.0 * float(B[r])))
    for r in range(m):
        ar, br, qr = float(A[r]), float(B[r]), float(qv[r])
        for s in range(r + 1, m):
            as_, bs, qs = float(A[s]), float(B[s]), float(qv[s])
            db = br - bs
            da = ar - as_
            dq = qr - qs
            if abs(db) < 1e-14:
                if abs(da) > 1e-14:
                    cands.append(-dq / da)
            else:
                disc = da * da - 4.0 * db * dq
                if disc >= -1e-12:
                    sqrt = math.sqrt(max(0.0, disc))
                    den = 2.0 * db
                    cands.append((-da + sqrt) / den)
                    cands.append((-da - sqrt) / den)
    best_a, best_pred = 0.0, 1e100
    for raw in cands:
        a = float(raw)
        if not math.isfinite(a):
            continue
        if a < lo:
            a = lo
        elif a > hi:
            a = hi
        aa = a * a
        p = float(qv[0]) + float(A[0]) * a + float(B[0]) * aa
        for r in range(1, m):
            v = float(qv[r]) + float(A[r]) * a + float(B[r]) * aa
            if v > p:
                p = v
        if p < best_pred:
            best_pred, best_a = p, a
    return best_a, best_pred

def local_sweep(h, q, grad, rowsF, rowsS, cols, pair_i, pair_j, act, Cq, rng, limit, off, mact):
    n = len(h); L = len(q)
    Aact = np.empty(mact, float); Bact = np.empty(mact, float)
    Afull = np.empty(L, float); tmp = np.empty(L, float); cand = np.empty(L, float)
    improved = False
    accepted = 0
    order = rng.permutation(len(pair_i))
    factors = (1.0, 0.5, 0.2, 0.05)
    for idx in order:
        if time.time() > limit:
            break
        i = int(pair_i[idx]); j = int(pair_j[idx])
        u = float(h[i]); v = float(h[j])
        lo = -min(u, 1.0 - v); hi = min(1.0 - u, v)
        if hi - lo < 1e-11:
            continue
        p1 = off + i - j; p2 = off + j - i
        tolq = max(1e-3, 1e-4 * Cq)
        qmax = float(q[act[0]])
        am = 0
        for r in range(mact):
            if float(q[act[r]]) >= qmax - tolq:
                am += 1
            else:
                break
        if am < 1:
            am = 1
        active = act[:am]
        Aact[:am] = grad[active, i]
        Aact[:am] -= grad[active, j]
        qv = q[active]
        Bact[:am] = 0.0
        for r in range(am):
            p = int(active[r])
            if p == off:
                Bact[r] = -2.0
            if p == p1:
                Bact[r] += 1.0
            if p == p2:
                Bact[r] += 1.0
        a, pred = choose_active_alpha(Aact[:am], qv, Bact[:am], lo, hi)
        if pred >= Cq - 1e-12 or abs(a) < 1e-15:
            continue
        np.subtract(grad[:, i], grad[:, j], out=Afull)
        for f in factors:
            aa = a * f
            if abs(aa) < 1e-15:
                continue
            a2 = aa * aa
            tmp[:] = Afull
            tmp *= aa
            tmp[off] -= 2.0 * a2
            tmp[p1] += a2
            tmp[p2] += a2
            np.add(q, tmp, out=cand)
            new_idx = int(cand.argmax())
            newC = float(cand[new_idx])
            if newC < Cq - 1e-13:
                q += tmp
                h[i] += aa; h[j] -= aa
                grad[rowsF[i], cols] -= aa
                grad[rowsS[i], cols] -= aa
                grad[rowsF[j], cols] += aa
                grad[rowsS[j], cols] += aa
                Cq = newC
                improved = True
                accepted += 1
                if (accepted & 15) == 0 or int(act[0]) != new_idx:
                    act = recompute_act(q, L, mact)
                if accepted % 5000 == 0:
                    q[:] = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
                    Cq = float(q.max())
                    grad[:] = build_grad(h, n)
                    act = recompute_act(q, L, mact)
                break
    return act, Cq, improved

def sa_sweep(h, q, grad, rowsF, rowsS, cols, rng, T0, Tmin, end_limit, off):
    n = len(h); L = len(q); dx = 2.0 / n
    Cq = float(q.max())
    tmp = np.empty(L, float); cand = np.empty(L, float)
    t0 = time.time(); total = max(1.0, end_limit - t0)
    rec = 0
    while time.time() < end_limit:
        prog = (time.time() - t0) / total
        if prog > 1.0:
            prog = 1.0
        T = T0 * (1.0 - prog) * (1.0 - prog) + Tmin
        i = rng.randint(n); j = rng.randint(n)
        if i == j:
            continue
        u = float(h[i]); v = float(h[j])
        lo = -min(u, 1.0 - v); hi = min(1.0 - u, v)
        gap = hi - lo
        if gap < 1e-10:
            continue
        r = rng.random()
        if r < 0.7:
            a = lo + gap * rng.random()
        elif r < 0.9:
            a = (rng.random() * 2.0 - 1.0) * 0.1 * gap
            if a < lo:
                a = lo
            elif a > hi:
                a = hi
        else:
            a = (rng.random() * 2.0 - 1.0) * 0.02
            if a < lo:
                a = lo
            elif a > hi:
                a = hi
        p1 = off + i - j; p2 = off + j - i
        np.subtract(grad[:, i], grad[:, j], out=tmp)
        a2 = a * a
        tmp *= a
        tmp[off] -= 2.0 * a2
        tmp[p1] += a2
        tmp[p2] += a2
        np.add(q, tmp, out=cand)
        new_idx = int(cand.argmax())
        newC = float(cand[new_idx])
        accept = False
        if newC < Cq:
            accept = True
        else:
            dC = (newC - Cq) * dx
            if dC <= 0.0:
                accept = True
            else:
                ratio = dC / T
                if ratio < 700.0 and rng.random() < math.exp(-ratio):
                    accept = True
        if accept:
            q += tmp
            h[i] += a; h[j] -= a
            grad[rowsF[i], cols] -= a
            grad[rowsS[i], cols] -= a
            grad[rowsF[j], cols] += a
            grad[rowsS[j], cols] += a
            Cq = newC
            rec += 1
            if rec & 4095 == 0:
                q[:] = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
                Cq = float(q.max())
                grad[:] = build_grad(h, n)
    return Cq

def alternate_starts(h, n, rng):
    res = []
    if n <= 0:
        return res
    th = np.zeros(n)
    th[np.argsort(h)[::-1][:n // 2]] = 1.0
    res.append((th, n))
    if n % 2 == 0:
        m = n // 2
        first = h[:m].copy()
        res.append((project_feasible(np.concatenate((first, first)), n), n))
        res.append((project_feasible(np.concatenate((first, 1.0 - first)), n), n))
    if n > 140:
        m = n // 2
        xf = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
        xc = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, m, endpoint=False)
        res.append((project_feasible(np.interp(xc, xf, h), m), m))
    return res

def refine(h_start, n, deadline, rng, T0_base=0.002, local_limit=25.0, sa_chunk=25.0, mact=8):
    h = project_feasible(h_start, n)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    q = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    L = len(q)
    off = n - 1
    Cq = float(q.max())
    grad = build_grad(h, n)
    mact = min(mact, max(1, L))
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_C5 = Cq * dx
    cols = np.arange(n)
    rowsF = off + cols[None, :] - cols[:, None]
    rowsS = off + cols[:, None] - cols[None, :]
    pair_i, pair_j = np.triu_indices(n, 1)
    act = recompute_act(q, L, mact)
    stagnant = 0
    while time.time() < deadline - 8.0:
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        ll = min(local_limit, rem - 5.0)
        if ll < 1.0:
            break
        act, Cq, improved = local_sweep(h, q, grad, rowsF, rowsS, cols, pair_i, pair_j, act, Cq, rng, time.time() + ll, off, mact)
        C5 = Cq * dx
        if C5 < best_C5 - 1e-15:
            best_C5 = C5
            best_h = h.copy()
            stagnant = 0
        elif not improved:
            stagnant += 1
        if time.time() >= deadline - 8.0:
            break
        if not improved or stagnant >= 3:
            if deadline - time.time() > 20.0:
                T0 = min(0.03, max(0.0005, T0_base * (1.5 ** stagnant)))
                Cq = sa_sweep(h, q, grad, rowsF, rowsS, cols, rng, T0, 1e-5, min(deadline - 8.0, time.time() + sa_chunk), off)
                C5 = Cq * dx
                if C5 > best_C5 + 1e-12:
                    h = best_h.copy()
                    q = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
                    Cq = float(q.max())
                    grad = build_grad(h, n)
                    C5 = best_C5
                else:
                    best_C5 = C5
                    best_h = h.copy()
                stagnant = 0
    if time.time() < deadline - 2.0:
        act = recompute_act(q, L, mact)
        local_sweep(h, q, grad, rowsF, rowsS, cols, pair_i, pair_j, act, Cq, rng, deadline - 2.0, off, mact)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c5 = c5_value(h)
    bh = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    bc = c5_value(bh)
    if bc < c5:
        return bh, bc
    return h, c5

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h0 = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = int(len(h0))
    except Exception:
        n0 = 250
        h0 = 0.5 + 0.25 * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * np.arange(n0) / float(n0))
    if n0 < 2:
        n0 = 250
        h0 = 0.5 + 0.25 * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * np.arange(n0) / float(n0))
    if n0 > 600:
        m = 300
        xf = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n0, endpoint=False)
        xc = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, m, endpoint=False)
        h0 = np.interp(xc, xf, h0)
        n0 = m
    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n0)
    best_h = h0
    best_n = n0
    best_c = c5_value(h0)
    total = max(10.0, deadline - t0)
    first_end = min(deadline, t0 + max(30.0, total * 0.40))
    h, c = refine(h0, n0, first_end, rng, T0_base=0.002, local_limit=25.0, sa_chunk=25.0)
    if c < best_c:
        best_h, best_n, best_c = h, n, c
    for hst, nst in alternate_starts(best_h, best_n, rng):
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 90.0:
            break
        alloc = min(140.0, max(35.0, rem * 0.22))
        end = min(deadline, time.time() + alloc)
        h, c = refine(hst, nst, end, rng, T0_base=0.008, local_limit=12.0, sa_chunk=12.0)
        if c < best_c:
            best_h, best_n, best_c = h, nst, c
    if deadline - time.time() > 25.0:
        h, c = refine(best_h, best_n, deadline - 2.0, rng, T0_base=0.0008, local_limit=20.0, sa_chunk=10.0)
        if c < best_c:
            best_h, best_n, best_c = h, best_n, c
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = c5_value(best_h)
    return best_h, best_c, best_n
```
